This enables FiUni to adaptively determine whether to reuse existing knowledge, expand a related subspace, or create a new subspace, dynamically balancing knowledge sharing and task isolation. Experiments show that FiUni can effectively infer latent batch-level task affiliations and achieve competitive performance against advanced task-aware CL methods with fewer trainable parameters.

</details>


### [271] [Emotional Preferences as Goal-Priority Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27072)
*Shiqi Liu,Yihua Tan,Hu Fu,Guanyu Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过强化学习实现"涌现情感偏好"的框架，让智能体的高层目标自主生成对低层竞争性目标的相对偏好（而非外部预设），并在多目标探索环境中验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：智能体决策中，竞争性低层目标的相对优先级是否能由高层目标自主生成的情感偏好决定，而非外部预设。在动态外部环境和内部状态变化下，情感在调节竞争目标相对优先级中起重要功能作用，需探索如何在计算上实现这一机制。

Method: 提出涌现情感偏好概念，基于"多目标强化学习内控制器 + 外偏好生成器"框架。内控制器提供偏好条件化的目标导向行为库；外偏好生成器通过高层目标的强化学习学习从当前状态到目标偏好的映射。并推导了偏好调节策略空间的策略优化差距上界。

Result: 在自建多目标探索环境中，学习到的偏好函数表现出情境性优先级切换、梯度权衡和时间持续性，优于固定偏好和手工设计偏好的策略。

Conclusion: 情感偏好可以通过优化自然地涌现为状态依赖的目标优先级调节机制，当最优策略可由偏好条件化策略表示时，最优性差距趋近于零，验证了该方法从高层目标自主调节低层目标优先级的可行性。

Abstract: A core question in decision-making for agents is whether the relative priorities of competing lower-level objectives can be determined by emotional preferences autonomously generated by higher-level goals, rather than being externally prespecified. Under changing external environments and evolving internal states, emotions play an important functional role in regulating the relative priorities of competing goals. Inspired by the goal-directed theory of emotion, this paper studies how such preference regulation can be computationally realized through reinforcement learning. We first propose a conception of emergent emotional preference: a high-level goal autonomously induces state-dependent preferences over competing lower-level objectives. This conception is built upon a framework consisting of a multi-objective reinforcement learning inner controller and an outer preference generator. The inner controller provides a repertoire of preference-conditioned goal-directed behaviors, while the outer preference generator learns a mapping from the current state to objective preferences through reinforcement learning on a high-level goal. We operationalize emotional preference as a state-dependent regulation of relative goal priorities that emerges through optimization. Furthermore, we characterize the policy space induced by preference regulation and derive an upper bound on the optimality gap in terms of the representation error of the inner behavioral repertoire. We show that the gap vanishes when the optimal policy can be represented by the available preference-conditioned policies. Experiments in self-constructed multi-objective exploration environments show that the learned preference function exhibits contextual priority switching, graded trade-offs, and temporal persistence, and outperforms the evaluated fixed-preference and handcrafted-preference strategies.

</details>


### [272] [Tabular Deep Learning for Algorithmic Trading: Cross-Regime Bayesian Optimisation for Equity Signal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27076)
*Joshua Le Grice*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在约300只美国大盘股、11年的日度数据上，对五类预测模型进行贝叶斯超参数优化，专门以跨市场机制的稳健性为优化目标。结果显示机制稳健的超参数选择能带来样本外泛化，没有单一表格深度学习方法超越梯度提升树，但通过秩聚合组合XGBoost与TabNet的Hybrid集成实现了年化51.26%、夏普比率2.44、CAPM alpha 0.423（p=0.011）的显著表现，且beta接近零说明收益源于选股而非市场暴露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法交易市场规模已超200亿美元，信号稳健性的微小增益即可转化为显著的经济回报。然而现有股票预测模型评估在超参数选择过程中并未明确针对市场机制（regime）的稳健性加以优化，这一空白可能导致模型在特定市场状态下过拟合或失效。

Method: 在约300只大盘股、11年日度数据上，训练五类模型（含表格深度学习和梯度提升树等），使用贝叶斯优化，其目标设定为在三个统计上显著不同的市场机制下优化交易表现。通过秩聚合（rank aggregation）方式将XGBoost与TabNet组合为Hybrid集成模型。

Result: 机制稳健的超参数选择与样本外泛化相关，信号精度在测试期四个季度中均高于随机基线；组合模型在输入噪声下表现缓慢退化，越过阈值后崩溃。没有单一表格深度学习方法超越XGBoost等梯度提升树，但Hybrid集成达到年化51.26%的收益率、夏普比率2.44、CAPM alpha 0.423（p=0.011），beta接近零。另发现替代数据在技术和基本面特征之外贡献有限，且空头贡献强于多头，效果因模型类别而异。

Conclusion: 以跨市场机制稳健性为目标的超参数选择能够有效提升样本外泛化能力；梯度提升树仍具有竞争力，但通过集成策略可超越单一模型，获得显著且由选股驱动的超额收益。替代数据作为辅助信号具有模型依赖性和多空不对称性。交互式应用已实现实时探索，剩余的关键步骤是接入实时数据以推进实际部署。

Abstract: Algorithmic trading now represents a market exceeding $20 billion, where even marginal gains in signal robustness can translate into economically significant returns. Existing evaluations of equity prediction models do not explicitly target regime robustness during hyperparameter selection. Five model classes are trained on daily observations from approximately 300 large-cap US equities over eleven years, with Bayesian optimisation configured to target trading performance across three statistically different market regimes. Regime-robust hyperparameter selection is associated with out-of-sample generalisation, as signal precision remains above the random baseline across all four quarters of the test period, and portfolio performance slowly degrades under simulated input noise before collapsing beyond a defined threshold. No individual tabular deep learning architecture outperforms gradient-boosted trees, but combining XGBoost and TabNet using rank aggregation produces a Hybrid ensemble with an annualised return of 51.26%, a Sharpe ratio of 2.44, and a statistically significant CAPM alpha of 0.423 (p = 0.011). A near-zero beta indicates this outperformance is driven by stock selection, not market exposure. Alternative data plays a secondary role once technical and fundamental features are accounted for, as well as contributing more strongly on the short side than the long, and varies by model class. An interactive application makes these results explorable in real time, with live data integration the remaining step toward practical deployment.

</details>


### [273] [Cone Extended Rayleigh Quotients for Directed Graph Learning: Minimax Spectral Certificates, Sensitivity, and Adaptive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27122)
*Yavdat Sh. Il'yasov,Nur F. Valeev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向有向图学习的频谱认证、灵敏度分析和控制方法论，基于双面锥Rayleigh框架处理广义铅笔B_θ-λG，无需对称性、非负性或锥保持假设。通过可计算的锥上下界实现后验包络，并引入光滑soft-min/max代理保持严格单侧界及可微性。实验表明该方法在非锥保持算子和有向学习场景中有效，并在Cora引用网络上实现约21.5%的频谱水平降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有向图学习自然产生非对称传播算子，其左右频谱结构不同，而传统方法通常依赖对称性、非负性或锥保持等严格假设，限制了在有向和非锥保持场景中的应用。需要一种无需这些约束的频谱认证、灵敏度分析和控制框架。

Method: 基于双面锥Rayleigh框架分析广义铅笔B_θ-λG；在正象限设置下利用可计算的锥上下界获得锥水平的后验包络；使用光滑soft-min/max代理函数保持严格单侧界同时保证对可训练参数的可微性；推导简单内部水平下右左模式的一阶灵敏度公式Dλ_C(B)[H]=v_C^T H u_C，用于图支持干预和自适应频谱控制。

Result: 数值实验证明方法适用于非锥保持算子和有向学习设置；符号非对称扰动展示从内部特征对到边界互补准对的转变；受控实验表明对称化可移除仅由边方向携带的预测信息。在Cora引用网络上，自适应重计算左右灵敏度在0.5%累积边权重缩减预算下将频谱水平降低约21.5%，且测试准确率无变化。

Conclusion: 该框架为有向图学习中的频谱属性提供了无需对称假设的完整方法论，兼具严格的认证边界、可微的灵敏度分析和有效的自适应控制，同时揭示了边方向信息在预测中的不可替代性。

Abstract: Directed graph learning naturally leads to trainable nonsymmetric propagation operators with distinct right and left spectral structures. Building on the two-sided cone Rayleigh framework for generalized pencils \[ B_θ-λG, \] we develop a learning-oriented methodology for spectral certification, sensitivity analysis, and control without requiring symmetry, nonnegativity, or cone preservation. In the positive-orthant setting, computable lower and upper cone bounds provide an a posteriori enclosure of a distinguished cone level, while smooth soft-min/max surrogates preserve rigorous one-sided bounds with explicit approximation errors and remain differentiable with respect to the trainable parameters.
  For a simple interior level, the right and left modes satisfy \[ Dλ_C(B)[H]=v_C^T H u_C, \] yielding first-order optimal graph-supported interventions under prescribed perturbation budgets and motivating adaptive spectral control. Numerical experiments demonstrate the applicability of the approach beyond cone-preserving operators and in directed learning settings. Signed nonsymmetric perturbations reveal a transition from interior eigenpairs to boundary complementary quasi-pairs, including non-spectral cone levels, while controlled experiments show that symmetrization can remove predictive information carried solely by edge direction.
  On the directed Cora citation network, adaptive recomputation of the right--left sensitivity reduces the distinguished spectral level by approximately $21.5\%$ under a cumulative edge-weight reduction budget of $0.5\%$, with no observed change in test accuracy for the trained model and data split considered.

</details>


### [274] [TRACE-CRC: Trajectory-Adaptive Conformal Risk Control for Multi-Step Channel State Information Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27124)
*Kiarash Rezaei,Mehdi Sattari,Javad Aliakbari,Tommy Svensson,Paolo Monti,Carlos Natalino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TRACE-CRC方法，结合轨迹自适应校准和误差画像与共形风险控制，为多步CSI预测提供轨迹级的不确定性量化，在保持轨迹级覆盖的同时生成更小的不确定性球。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习CSI预测器只提供点预测，缺乏校准的不确定性估计，在多步CSI预测场景中，如果轨迹任意部分不可靠，下游波束成形或调度决策可能失败，亟需轨迹感知的不确定性量化方法。

Method: TRACE-CRC方法包括三个核心组件：一是基于未来步相关误差画像（future-step-dependent error profiling），二是轨迹难度分层（trajectory difficulty stratification），三是learn-then-test (LTT)风险控制。该方法构造Frobenius范数不确定性球包围预测的CSI矩阵，并控制至少一个未来帧未被覆盖的风险。

Result: 实验表明TRACE-CRC实现了可靠的轨迹级覆盖率，且不确定性球显著小于保守的多步校正方法，同时避免了紧凑逐步行和自适应共形基线的轨迹欠覆盖问题。

Conclusion: TRACE-CRC为多步CSI预测提供了一种有效且校准的轨迹级不确定性量化方案，平衡了覆盖可靠性与不确定性球紧致性之间的矛盾。

Abstract: Reliable prediction of time-varying channel state information (CSI) is essential for efficient wireless communication. Each CSI frame is a matrix-valued representation of the wireless channel response, and a sequence of CSI frames forms a temporal channel trajectory. Modern deep learning-based CSI predictors, however, often provide only point predictions and lack calibrated uncertainty estimates. This limitation is particularly problematic in multi-step CSI prediction, where the target is a sequence of future CSI matrices, and downstream decisions such as beamforming or scheduling may fail if any part of the predicted trajectory is unreliable. We propose trajectory-adaptive calibration and error profiling with conformal risk control (TRACE-CRC), a method for trajectory-aware uncertainty quantification in multi-step CSI prediction. TRACE-CRC constructs Frobenius-norm uncertainty balls around predicted CSI matrices and controls the risk that at least one future frame is uncovered. Instead of calibrating each future step independently, TRACE-CRC combines future-step-dependent error profiling, trajectory difficulty stratification, and learn-then-test (LTT) risk control. Empirically, TRACE-CRC achieves reliable trajectory-level coverage with substantially smaller uncertainty balls than conservative multi-step corrections, while avoiding the trajectory undercoverage of compact stepwise and adaptive conformal baselines.

</details>


### [275] [Ultra Low-Power, Lightweight, Probabilistic RSS-Based Path Reconstruction: A System for Landscape-Scale Bee Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27152)
*Christopher J. Noroozi,Joseph L. Woodgate,Michael Mangan,Michael T. Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于RSS的新型低功耗定位方法，通过旋转高增益发射机进行少量RSS测量，结合高斯过程与双随机变分推断，实现对超轻量级移动接收器（38mg）约15m精度的定位追踪，功耗低于180µW，并可扩展到约10m精度/600µW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在运动生态学、物联网和机器人等领域，需要为过小且功耗受限、无法实现GNSS的设备提供定位。现有基于RSS推断AoA的低功耗方法受限于范围和大量RSS测量带来的功耗，亟需一种更高效、更节能的定位方案。

Method: 使用简单旋转高增益发射机（覆盖300m范围）进行极少量RSS测量，通过概率建模推断AoA；随后用高斯过程建模接收器运动轨迹，并采用双随机变分推断进行轨迹重建。接收器重量仅38mg（含电源），通过调整RSS测量数量可权衡精度与功耗。

Result: 在可扩展的景观范围内实现对38mg（含电源）接收器的约15m定位精度，功耗低于180µW；增加RSS测量后精度提升至约10m，功耗低于600µW。系统实际应用于追踪Bombus terrestris（大地熊蜂）归巢飞行路线。

Conclusion: 该方法为超轻量级、超低功耗设备提供了一种有效且可扩展的定位追踪方案，有望支撑诸如飞行昆虫行为研究等领域，实际验证展示了其在真实生态场景中的可行性。

Abstract: Applications in fields such as movement ecology, Internet of Things or robotics share the need for systems that localize devices that are too small and power constrained to implement GNSS (Global Navigation Satellite Systems). Alternative low-power localization methods often rely on only measurements of RSS (Received Signal Strength) to infer the AoA (Angle of Arrival) of a transmitted radio frequency signal, but are limited by range and the power demand of the large number of RSS measurements required to infer an accurate AoA. In this paper we address these issues with a novel RSS-based method for tracking ultra lightweight and low-power moving receivers across a complex landscape, achieved by using a minimal number of RSS measurements from simple rotating high-gain transmitters with a range of 300m, and applying probabilistic modelling to infer their AoA. The receiver's movement path is then modelled using a Gaussian process and reconstructed using doubly stochastic variational inference, resulting in approximately 15m accuracy tracking of receivers weighing 38mg (including power source) over a scalable landscape range while consuming less than 180uW, increased to approximately 10m accuracy at less than 600uW by taking more RSS measurements. We anticipate that this method will support fields such as the behavioural study of flying insect species, which we demonstrate by applying the system to track Bombus terrestris nest return flights.

</details>


### [276] [Inductive Correlation Clustering with Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27153)
*Francesco Paolo Nerini,Francesco Bonchi,Arijit Khan,André Panisson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用图神经网络（GNN）解决归纳式相关聚类（Inductive Correlation Clustering）问题，这是对传统相关聚类（CC）的推广，能够处理未见过的图实例。该框架通过学习训练时的常见结构模式和节点特征，以极小计算开销泛化到同分布的新图，推理速度提升最高5个数量级，且近似比保持在最佳基线方案的10%以内，并可作为可学习的图分类池化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相关聚类算法存在两个核心局限：一是大规模问题上的可扩展性严重不足；二是算法本质上是直推式的（transductive），即每遇到一个新实例都必须从头执行算法，无法利用以往经验。

Method: 利用图神经网络（GNN）引入归纳式相关聚类（Inductive Correlation Clustering）这一新问题设定，通过在学习阶段捕捉图中的常见结构模式和节点特征，使框架能够泛化到来自同一分布的新图实例。此外，该方法还被用作可学习的图池化机制，用于图分类任务。

Result: 在归纳设置下，推理时间降低最多5个数量级，同时维持与最佳基线解决方案约10%以内的近似比；在标准（直推式）相关聚类基准上也取得了有竞争力的结果；作为GNN池化层时能够增强网络捕获层次化结构信息的能力。

Conclusion: 该框架成功弥合了相关聚类在可扩展性和泛化性方面的不足，既能在归纳场景中高效推理，也能在传统直推式基准上保持竞争力，同时展示了作为图分类池化机制的额外实用价值。

Abstract: Correlation Clustering (CC) is a natural formulation of clustering in combinatorial optimization, which uses a graph representation of the input and does not require a pre-specified number of clusters. Given $n$ objects and a pairwise similarity function, the goal is to cluster the objects so that similar objects are put in the same cluster and dissimilar objects are put in different clusters. Despite its versatility, existing CC algorithms suffer from significant scalability issues and are inherently transductive: i.e., the algorithm must be executed from scratch for any new problem instance.
  In this work, we bridge this gap by leveraging Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) to solve Inductive Correlation Clustering, a novel generalization of the CC problem designed to handle unseen graph instances. By learning to exploit common structural patterns and node features during training, our framework generalizes to new graphs drawn from the same distribution with minimal computational overhead with respect to standard algorithms. We demonstrate the effectiveness and scalability of our approach through extensive experiments. Our framework not only excels in the inductive setting, e.g., lowering the inference time up to $5$ order of magnitude, while maintaining an approximation ratio within $~10\%$ of the best baseline solution, but also achieves competitive results on standard (transductive) CC benchmarks. Finally, we showcase a practical application of our framework as a learnable pooling mechanism for graph classification. Our results indicate that our method serves as an efficient pooling layer, enhancing the ability of GNNs to capture hierarchical structural information in networks.

</details>


### [277] [Diffusion Policies for Short-Horizon Planning in Robot Crowd Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27158)
*Wendong Li,Jochen Garcke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PDPO框架，利用扩散策略生成短时域动作块进行机器人人群导航，并通过离线预训练+在线PPO微调的方式提升导航安全性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在人群导航中每时间步只输出单一反应性动作，无法充分表示多样化的短期避障策略，限制了在密集、动态、多模态人机交互场景中的决策质量。

Method: PDPO采用离线到在线的强化学习框架：使用扩散策略生成短时域（五步）动作块，先在碰撞避免演示数据上预训练，再通过PPO在线微调，将去噪过程视为内部决策过程；执行时以滚动时域方式应用动作块。同时引入边界约束设置，将越界视为碰撞以消除评估伪影。

Result: 实验表明PDPO在成功率上优于强基线，消融实验证明动作块在改进的有界基准中尤为关键。

Conclusion: 扩散策略驱动的动作块生成方式能够有效提升人群导航决策的多样性与成功率，边界约束的引入有助于更公平地评估导航算法性能。

Abstract: Robot crowd navigation requires safe and efficient decision-making under dense, dynamic, and multimodal human--robot interactions. Existing reinforcement-learning methods typically output a single reactive action at each timestep, which limits their ability to represent diverse short-term avoidance strategies. We propose Planning Diffusion Policy Optimization (PDPO), an offline-to-online reinforcement-learning framework that uses a diffusion policy to generate short-horizon action chunks for crowd navigation. PDPO is first pretrained on collision-avoidance demonstrations and then fine-tuned online with PPO by treating the denoising process as an internal decision process. During execution, the policy generates a five-step action chunk and applies it in a receding-horizon manner. Furthermore, we observe an evaluation artifact in common crowd-navigation benchmarks: without explicit boundary constraints, learned agents may leave the valid domain and bypass dense crowds. To address this, we introduce a setting in which boundary violations are treated as collisions. Experiments show that PDPO obtains an improved success rate over strong baselines, and ablations demonstrate that action chunks are especially important for the modified bounded benchmark.

</details>


### [278] [TraceBench: Controlled Evaluation of LLM Agents for Time-Series Root-Cause Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27182)
*Tommaso Bendinelli,Artur Dox,Christian Holz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TraceBench，一个基于仿真的框架，用于在受控条件下生成根因归因任务，并系统评估了四种LLM智能体在时间序列异常检测和根因分析上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体被越来越多地应用于真实系统的异常检测和根因分析，但其在受控条件下的性能尚未被系统评估。

Method: 提出TraceBench仿真框架，通过模拟物理动态系统生成受控的根因归因任务。每个任务中，智能体接收时间序列观测，需判断系统参数是否在模拟过程中被改变以及具体哪个参数被改变。从三个可解释的机械系统生成任务，在受控实验条件下系统评估四种LLM智能体。

Result: 智能体在获得领域上下文时表现显著提升，主要通过数值控制台输出而非可视化探索数据；当要求生成Python脚本将每个时间序列样本映射到预测的根因标签时，智能体表现普遍不如直接提交预测。

Conclusion: TraceBench为LLM智能体在动态系统时间序列分析中的能力提供了系统化的评估基准，揭示了领域上下文的重要性、数值探索偏好以及预测格式对性能的影响。数据集、智能体轨迹、实验结果和排行榜已在tracebench.github.io公开。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly applied to anomaly detection and root-cause analysis in time-series observations collected from real-world systems; however, their performance on these tasks has not been systematically evaluated under controlled conditions. We introduce TraceBench, a simulation-based framework for generating controlled root-cause attribution tasks. In each generated task, an agent receives time-series observations produced by simulating a physical dynamical system and must determine whether a system parameter was altered during the simulation and, if so, which one. Using TraceBench, we generate tasks from three interpretable mechanical systems and systematically evaluate four LLM agents across controlled experimental conditions, yielding new insights into how these agents analyze time-series observations from dynamical systems. Our results show that agents benefit substantially from domain context and explore data primarily through numerical console output rather than visualizations. We also find that agents generally perform worse when required to produce a Python script that maps each time-series sample to a predicted root-cause label than when they submit predictions directly. We release our datasets, agent trajectories, experimental results, and a leaderboard on our website, tracebench.github.io.

</details>


### [279] [When Interference Graphs Evolve: Doubly Robust Estimation of Dynamic Peer Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27187)
*Xiaojing Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个受控对比框架，用于在交互图演化场景下估计同伴效应，并开发了动态网络双重稳健估计器DynaNet-DR，通过时间分解倾向得分与归一化增强相结合，在半合成基准和MathOverflow观测研究上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当交互图随时间演化时，交互图演化难以估计同伴效应，因为分配前网络历史、动态同伴暴露和分配后网络变化具有不同的因果作用，现有方法未能清晰区分这些作用。

Method: 引入受控对比框架，用自身的处理、时间聚合的同伴暴露和分配后演化摘要来索引潜在结果，以此定义自身处理对比、同伴暴露对比、受控网络演化对比和联合受控对比。开发DynaNet-DR估计器，结合时间因子化倾向得分与归一化增强；实现中加入代表分数预测、固定裁剪和有限样本稳定化。

Result: 在固定真实时间图序列的半合成基准上，该方法在针对完整配置的方法中展现出良好的估计精度。在一致性、摘要充分性、序列可交换性、正性、扰动收敛和弱依赖条件下，标准估计器在结果回归或倾向得分估计器之一一致时具有一致性。

Conclusion: 该方法为图演化场景下的同伴效应估计提供了统一的因果框架，区分了多种对比而非中介分解；基准评估的是摘要索引对比而非反事实边的生成；MathOverflow研究作为观测性示例在既定假设下展示了应用价值。

Abstract: Peer effects are difficult to estimate when interaction graphs evolve because pre-assignment network history, dynamic peer exposure, and post-assignment network change have distinct causal roles. We introduce a controlled contrast framework that indexes potential outcomes by own treatment, temporally aggregated peer exposure, and a post-assignment evolution summary. Differences between the resulting means define own-treatment, peer-exposure, controlled network-evolution, and joint controlled contrasts rather than a mediation decomposition. We develop the Dynamic Network Doubly Robust estimator, DynaNet-DR, which combines a temporally factorized propensity with normalized augmentation. Under consistency, summary sufficiency, sequential exchangeability, positivity, nuisance convergence, and weak dependence, its canonical estimator is consistent when either the outcome regression or the propensity estimator is consistent. The reported implementation adds representative-score prediction, fixed clipping, and finite-sample stabilization. Semi-synthetic benchmarks on fixed real temporal graph sequences show favorable estimation accuracy among methods targeting the full profile. These benchmarks assess summary-indexed contrasts rather than counterfactual edge generation, and the MathOverflow study is an observational illustration under the stated assumptions.

</details>


### [280] [Common Geodesics Do Not Guarantee Fisher Consistency of the Structured SVM: Minimal Counterexamples and a Tree-Metric Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27203)
*Jintao Fei,Jiangying Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了结构化支持向量机的已知Fisher一致性必要条件（任务损失为满足公共测地线条件的度量）对坐标式argmax解码器而言并不充分，并给出了最小反例及完整的树度量分类结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化支持向量机的Fisher一致性需要任务损失满足特定条件（度量且每三个输出存在公共测地线），但该条件对坐标式argmax解码器是否充分此前未知。作者希望通过构造反例和分类来澄清该必要条件与argmax一致性之间的实际差距。

Method: 通过构造具体反例（四输出的单位星图、五输出的K_{2,3}二分图及三维Hamming立方体），利用精确原始-对偶证书验证最优性，并对满足公共测地线条件的树度量给出完整的充分必要条件分类（一致性当且仅当树为路径），同时讨论边界分布与满支撑分布的差异。

Result: 证明了公共测地线条件对argmax一致性不充分：四输出单位星存在精确最优得分向量所有argmax均为非贝叶斯分类器，且四输出是满足该条件的最小反例。树度量分类表明仅路径树具备argmax一致性；分支树失败仅限于边界分布，满支撑分布下所有树均保持argmax性质。五输出是满支撑反例的最小规模，K_{2,3}是无限K_{m,n}族的最小成员；三维Hamming立方体也存在满支撑反例。

Conclusion: 在多面体设定下，嵌入仅能保证存在校准链接，但无法保证预设的argmax链接在每个代理风险最小化点上有效，说明存在具体的解码器差距，需要更精细的条件来刻画结构化SVM的argmax一致性。

Abstract: A known necessary condition for Fisher consistency of the structured support vector machine requires the task loss to be a metric for which every output triple has a common geodesic point. We show that this condition is not sufficient for the canonical coordinate-wise argmax decoder. A four-output unit star admits an exactly optimal score vector whose maximizers are all strictly non-Bayes, and four outputs are minimal among metrics satisfying the condition. We then completely classify positively weighted tree metrics whose vertex set is the output space: argmax consistency holds if and only if the tree is a path. The failure on branching trees is confined to boundary distributions; every tree retains the argmax property at every full-support distribution. Among metrics satisfying the common-geodesic condition, five outputs are necessary and sufficient for a full-support counterexample; $K_{2,3}$ is the smallest member of an infinite $K_{m,n}$ family. We additionally give a full-support counterexample for the three-dimensional Hamming cube. All optimality claims have exact primal-dual certificates. The counterexamples expose a concrete decoder gap: in this polyhedral setting, an embedding can guarantee the existence of a calibrated link without validating a prescribed argmax link on every surrogate-risk minimizer.

</details>


### [281] [Profit based evaluation of machine learning for nitrogen recommendations in winter wheat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27205)
*Xulong Wang,Po Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出以利润损失（而非预测准确率）作为氮肥施用量建议的评分标准。基于892条产量响应曲线构建测试平台，扫掠氮肥与谷物价格比，发现机器学习作为独立预测器表现不佳——没有模型能在农场容忍范围内恢复最优施氮量，且基准噪声证明模型本质不可能做到。但在正常价格下，所有模型输给标准建议的利润；而一个简单的校正步骤可将利润损失削减四分之一，且在不重新训练的情况下在第二站点削减43%的损失。标准建议加阻尼校正的混合方案可消除偏差并削减罕见大额损失。价格扫掠还可用于为减排定价，成本接近当前碳价。结论：机器学习应作为标准建议的利润评分校正器，而非其替代品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冬小麦的氮肥用量在季前设定，此时价格和天气未知。英国标准建议不响应价格变化，但近期价格波动使最优施氮量移动数十公斤/公顷。机器学习常被提出为解决方案，但通常仅以预测准确率评判，而准确预测本身并不使推荐率更有利可图。因此需要直接以利润损失来评分氮肥建议。

Method: 以某种能直接反映建议在实测产量响应曲线上所放弃的利润的方式给氮肥建议打分。构建包含来自两个长期英国试验的892条响应曲线的测试平台，扫掠氮肥对谷物的价格比以覆盖所有价格情景。在平台上评估多种机器学习模型，并应用简单校正步骤及混合策略（标准建议+阻尼校正）。

Result: 机器学习作为独立预测器失败：没有模型能在农场容忍范围内恢复最优施氮率，基准噪声表明任何模型都无法做到。在正常价格下所有模型在利润上均输给标准建议。简单校正步骤可使利润损失减少四分之一；更好的模型和额外特征无增益。同一冻结校正在第二站点不需重训练即可削减43%损失。混合（标准建议+阻尼校正）消除偏差并削减罕见大额损失。价格扫掠将减排成本定价为接近当前碳价。

Conclusion: 机器学习应作为利润评分的标准建议校正器付费使用，而不是作为标准建议的替代品。

Abstract: Nitrogen rates for winter wheat are set before the season, under unknown prices and weather. The standard UK advice does not respond to prices, yet recent price swings moved the most profitable rate by tens of kilograms per hectare. Machine learning is often proposed as the fix. However, it is usually judged on prediction accuracy, and accurate prediction does not by itself make the recommended rate more profitable. Our insight is to score nitrogen advice directly by the profit it forgoes on measured yield response curves. We build a test bench on 892 such curves from two long running UK experiments, and sweep the nitrogen to grain price ratio to cover all price scenarios. On this bench, machine learning fails as a predictor. No model recovers the best rate within farm tolerance, and the benchmark noise shows none can. At normal prices, every model also loses to the standard advice on profit. The gain sits elsewhere. A simple correction step applied after the model cuts profit losses by a quarter, while better models and extra features give no gain. The same frozen correction cuts losses by 43% at the second site without any retraining. A hybrid of standard advice plus a damped correction removes bias and trims rare large losses. The same price sweep also prices emission cuts, at a cost comparable to current carbon prices. Machine learning therefore pays as a profit scored correction to standard advice, not as its replacement.

</details>


### [282] [HALO: A Heterogeneity-Aware Language-Aligned IMU Foundation Model for Open-Set Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27233)
*Zihan Ding,Liyu Zhang,Xiaomin Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HALO是一个面向IMU（惯性测量单元）的领域特定基础模型，通过两阶段训练框架（异构感知自监督预训练+同义词感知软对比学习的语言对齐）同时解决传感异构性和开放集泛化两大挑战，在10个公开数据集上训练、7个held-out数据集上评估，以约35M参数量（比MOMENT少10倍）在全部8个聚合指标上超越5个SOTA基线，零样本开放集准确率提升13.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HAR领域缺乏能够跨主体、跨设备、跨活动泛化的统一模型。两大核心挑战阻碍了统一基础模型的训练：一是传感异构性（采样率、通道配置、传感器放置位置的差异）；二是对未见活动和标签词汇的泛化能力差。现有方法依赖逐数据集分类器，无法实现真正的开放集识别。

Method: 提出两阶段训练框架：Stage 1采用异构感知自监督学习预训练IMU编码器，包括自适应池化tokenization、通道独立特征提取、上下文感知传感器条件嵌入（将自然语言传感器描述注入每个通道嵌入）；Stage 2通过同义词感知软对比学习将IMU编码器与文本嵌入对齐，利用余弦相似度检索实现开放集识别，无需逐数据集分类器。

Result: 在10个公开HAR数据集上训练、7个held-out数据集上评估，HALO在全部8个聚合指标上超越5个SOTA基线；在基线匹配输入条件下仍在4个设置中的3个保持领先。仅使用约35M可训练参数（比MOMENT的341.2M少10倍），零样本开放集准确率（覆盖87个训练标签）提升13.7个百分点。在两个严重分布偏移的数据集上，包括HALO在内的所有模型零样本性能均崩溃。

Conclusion: HALO证明了一个轻量级、异构感知、语言对齐的IMU基础模型能够有效实现跨数据集和开放集的人类活动识别，且参数效率远优于现有基础模型。同时指出严重分布偏移场景下零样本性能的局限性仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Human Activity Recognition (HAR) using inertial measurement units (IMUs) enables a wide range of applications, yet the field still lacks a unified model that can generalize across diverse subjects, devices, and activities. Training such a model is difficult due to two key challenges: sensing heterogeneity -- differences in sampling rates, channel configurations, and sensor placements -- and poor generalization to unseen activities and label vocabularies. We introduce HALO (Heterogeneity-Aware Language-aligned Open-set model), a domain-specific IMU foundation model that addresses both challenges through a two-stage training framework. Stage 1 pretrains the IMU encoder with heterogeneity-aware self-supervised learning, including adaptive-pooling tokenization, channel-independent feature extraction, and contextualized sensor conditioning that injects natural-language sensor descriptions into each channel embedding. Stage 2 aligns this IMU encoder with text embeddings via synonym-aware soft contrastive learning, enabling open-set recognition via cosine-similarity retrieval without per-dataset classifiers. Trained on 10 public HAR datasets and evaluated on 7 held-out datasets, HALO outperforms five state-of-the-art baselines on all 8 aggregate metrics, and still leads on 3 of 4 settings under baseline-matched inputs. Despite using only ~35M trainable parameters -- 10x fewer than the latest foundation model MOMENT (341.2M) -- HALO improves zero-shot open-set accuracy, measured over all 87 training labels, by 13.7 percentage points. On two further datasets with severe distribution shift, every model including HALO collapses zero-shot. A video demonstration of HALO's performance in real world is available at https://youtu.be/rooVKragtFU

</details>


### [283] [Importance Scoring of Transformer Attention Heads in Learning Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27241)
*Ahmad Jad Allah,Kazi F. Akhter,Md. Kamrozzaman Bhuiyan,Manar D. Samad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将重要性评分指标应用于多头部Transformer模型在表格数据学习中的可解释性分析，通过在40个多样化表格数据集上的实验，证明基于头部重要性分数的头部剪枝具有鲁棒性，并揭示表格数据中注意力头部的重要性分布与图像和语言领域存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算密集且不透明的深度学习模型可以通过分析其数据变换方式来更好地理解和优化。尽管深层Transformer已在计算机视觉和自然语言处理领域被广泛研究，但其在表格数据上的应用仍相对未被充分探索，缺乏对Transformer内部机制（尤其是多头注意力）在表格学习场景下的可解释性分析。

Method: 提出一种重要性评分度量方法，用于解释多头部Transformer模型在表格数据学习中的行为。通过在40个多样化表格数据集上进行系统实验，该方法通过逐步剪枝注意力头部（分别按最低和最高重要性分数）来评估模型性能的鲁棒性变化，并分析不同注意力层中头部重要性分数的分布模式。

Result: 在72.5%的实验案例中，模型在逐步移除重要性分数最低的头部时表现出最大的鲁棒性，而首先移除重要性最高的注意力头部会导致分类性能最大幅度的下降。六个注意力层中发现重要头部分散在各个层中，没有一致的层特定趋势。与图像和语言领域不同，表格数据集中各个注意力头部的重要性因数据集的不同模式（schema）和特征空间而存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 所提出的头部重要性评分可以提升Transformer架构内部的效率和冗余性，为表格学习领域中的Transformer模型提供了一种有效的可解释性工具和优化方向。源代码已公开。

Abstract: Computationally demanding and opaque deep learning models can be better understood and optimized by analyzing how they transform data. While deep transformers have been widely studied in computer vision and natural language processing, their application in tabular data remains relatively underexplored. This paper presents one of the first applications of an importance-scoring metric to interpret multi-head transformer models in learning from tabular data. Experiments conducted on 40 diverse tabular datasets demonstrate robustness to head drops based on the proposed head importance score. In 72.5\% of experimental examples, the model remains most resilient to performance drops when heads with the lowest importance scores are gradually removed. In contrast, removing the most important attention head first results in the greatest reduction in classification performance. A closer look at individual head importance scores across six attention layers reveals that important heads are scattered across layers, with no consistent layer-specific trends. In contrast to the image and language domains, the importance of individual attention heads varies considerably across tabular datasets with different schemas and feature spaces. The proposed importance score can improve efficiency and redundancy within transformer architectures. We make the source code for measuring the importance of individual attention heads publicly available.

</details>


### [284] [Circuit Condensation: Post-Training that Concentrates a Behavior's Causal Circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27254)
*Sai Adith Senthil Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出电路压缩（Circuit Condensation）方法，通过后训练使模型行为集中在更小的因果图上，在四个行为、八个模型上相比最强冻结基线平均缩小8.1倍（最高316倍），且压缩后的电路能追踪原模型的下一个token分布并预测其错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性中的一个核心问题是电路发现往往返回数百条边，导致电路难以检查、比较和穷尽验证。现有冻结发现方法产生的大规模电路限制了可解释性分析的可行性，因此需要更小的因果图来集中表达模型行为。

Method: Circuit Condensation：每一轮先剪除低归因（low-attribution）的边，然后训练一个低秩适配器（low-rank adapter）通过剩余边来匹配原始模型输出；仅当任务性能和通用能力保持时保留该剪枝。通过迭代多轮压缩来将行为集中到更小的因果图中。

Result: 在32个设定中30个优于最强冻结基线，平均缩小8.1倍，最高316倍；无权重更新的重复搜索在32个设定中29个产生更大电路，证明权重更新是主要驱动因素。19个电路全子集测试发现11个不可再压缩，其余含可移除边；成对消融揭示了边之间的依赖关系。间接宾语识别任务上，压缩出24个注意力头（17个有文献记载的角色），而匹配的冻结电路需要61个头（36个未记载）。

Conclusion: 电路压缩能够生成比冻结发现明显更小的因果电路，这些电路不仅规模更小，而且是已发表机制的充分子电路而非重构，同时能追踪原始模型的输出分布并预测其错误，为机制可解释性提供了更可验证、可比较的电路表征。

Abstract: One approach to mechanistic interpretability explains behavior through circuits: the components and connections that carry it. Frozen discovery often returns hundreds of edges, making them hard to inspect, compare, or verify exhaustively. We introduce Circuit Condensation, which post-trains models to concentrate behaviors into smaller causal graphs. Each round prunes low-attribution edges and trains a low-rank adapter to match the original through what remains, retaining the cut only if task performance and general capability survive. Across four behaviors and eight models, condensed circuits are smaller than the strongest frozen baseline in 30 of 32 settings, by $8.1\times$ on average and up to $316\times$. Repeating the search without weight updates produces larger circuits in 29 of 32 settings, showing that weight updates, rather than search alone, drive the reduction. Testing every subset of 19 circuits finds 11 that cannot be reduced and reveals removable edges in the rest. Pair ablations expose dependencies between edges, showing that their effects cannot be understood independently. On indirect object identification, condensation isolates 24 heads, 17 of them with documented roles, against 61 heads and 36 undocumented ones for the matched frozen circuit: a sufficient sub-circuit of the published mechanism rather than a reconstruction of it. The resulting circuit tracks the original model's next-token distribution and predicts its errors.

</details>


### [285] [Making Latent Evolution Explicit: Operator-Structured Transitions for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27259)
*Xiaoxiao Lu,Yunlong Dong,Jiahao Shi,Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜空间演化算子网络（LEON），将WAM中的潜状态转移视为独立于预测表示和策略耦合的架构选择，通过基于受控Koopman生成器的算子传播与加性强迫建模潜演化，在多个WAM公式下提升闭环性能与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜空间世界动作模型（WAMs）使用Transformer类预测器实现潜状态转移，但其归纳结构侧重于token交互而非时间演化，未能充分利用动态系统的时间演化特性，因此需要将转移实现作为独立的架构选择进行专门研究。

Method: 提出Latent Evolution Operator Network（LEON），在可学习的可观测空间中通过上下文调制的算子传播和加性强迫实现潜演化建模；其理论基础为受控Koopman生成器视角，将依赖上下文的转移变化围绕共享的演化算子结构组织，同时保留加性变化的互补路径。

Result: 在受控动态系统上验证了演化专用归纳偏置以及算子传播与强迫的互补作用；在两种不同集成方式的WAM公式下，LEON均提升了闭环性能与鲁棒性，且在完全替换转移模块时依然有效。

Conclusion: 实验结果确立了转移实现在潜空间WAMs中是一个关键的架构选择，而非附属细节，为未来WAM设计提供了新的视角。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) augment robot policies by predicting how task-relevant scene states may evolve under interaction. Recent WAMs increasingly perform such prediction in latent representation spaces, avoiding full appearance-level generation while preserving control-relevant information. Yet latent transitions are commonly realized with Transformer-based predictors whose inductive structure is centered on token interaction rather than temporal evolution. We study transition realization as an architectural choice distinct from predictive representation and prediction-policy coupling. We introduce the Latent Evolution Operator Network (LEON), which models latent evolution in a learned observable space through context-modulated operator-based propagation and additive forcing. Grounded in the controlled Koopman generator view of evolution, LEON organizes context-dependent transition variation around a shared evolution-operator structure while retaining a complementary path for additive change. Controlled dynamical systems verify the resulting evolution-specific inductive bias and the complementary roles of operator propagation and forcing. Across two WAM formulations that integrate latent prediction into the policy differently, LEON improves closed-loop performance and robustness while remaining effective under full transition replacement. These results establish transition realization as a consequential architectural choice in latent WAMs.

</details>


### [286] [MM-Spectrum: Multimodal Multi-spectral Molecular Structural Elucidation with a Stable MoE Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27286)
*Hai-tao Yu,Nan Min,Zheng Fang,Hongyu Zhan,Yusen Tan,Yuhan Wang,Jun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了MM-Spectrum，一种面向多模态光谱-结构解析的稀疏专家混合（MoE）框架，通过显式的模态感知路由机制、共享与交互专家以及异构专家容量，解决多光谱模态不平衡导致的多模态融合性能退化问题，在多种模态设置下均取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态光谱测量整合互补但高度异构的信号进行分子结构推断时，直接将多光谱序列拼接会因模态间显著异质性和多模态不平衡而产生异常的性能退化，需要专门设计缓解该问题的融合框架。

Method: 提出MM-Spectrum稀疏专家混合框架：(1) 显式模态感知路由机制，将光谱身份信息暴露给路由器以匹配多光谱不平衡下的信息特性；(2) 引入共享专家和交互专家，配合异构专家容量，提取模态特有信息与跨模态协同信息，同时抑制噪声诱导干扰。

Result: 在全模态、双模态和缺失模态设置下的分子结构解析任务中，MM-Spectrum均取得一致且显著的性能提升，消融实验和可解释性分析进一步验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过模态感知路由与异构专家设计，MM-Spectrum有效缓解了多光谱模态不平衡问题，为多模态光谱到结构的解析提供了一种鲁棒且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Inferring molecular structures from multimodal spectroscopic measurements requires integrating complementary yet highly heterogeneous signals. However, the common paradigm of directly concatenating multispectral sequences can exhibit anomalous performance degradation, primarily due to pronounced heterogeneity and the resulting multimodal imbalance across modalities. As a remedy, we propose MM-Spectrum, a sparse Mixture-of-Experts framework tailored for multimodal multispectral spectra-to-structure elucidation. To better match the information characteristics under multispectral imbalance, MM-Spectrum introduces an explicit modality-aware routing mechanism that exposes spectral identity to the router in addition to token content representations. Moreover, it incorporates shared and interaction experts, together with heterogeneous expert capacities, to extract multispectral modality-unique and cross-modal synergistic information while suppressing noise-induced interference. Across full-modality, bimodal, and missing-modality settings on molecular structural elucidation, MM-Spectrum achieves consistent and substantial improvements, supported by ablation studies and interpretability analyses.

</details>


### [287] [QuantumBoostNet: A Hybrid Classical-Quantum Architecture for Enhanced Accuracy in Cardiac Ultrasound View Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27302)
*Mihai Udrescu-Milosav,Stefan-Alexandru Jura,Mihai Udrescu,Gerhard-Paul Diller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种名为QuantumBoostNet的混合经典-量子架构，用于心脏超声（超声心动图）的视图/角度识别任务。该模型整合经典骨干网络与经典/量子双头结构，其中量子头为10量子比特的参数化量子电路，通过两阶段训练和自适应混合参数过渡。实验表明其在心脏超声视图识别任务上优于现有最先进的经典和混合模型，并在图像分类基准上表现优异且具备噪声鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏超声中心脏视图/角度的准确识别是心脏病学成像的关键步骤，对精确解剖解释、可靠测量和减少临床错误至关重要。尽管计算机视觉已取得显著进展，但大多数最先进的模型在标准基准上表现良好，却因医学成像数据中高水平的噪声而在专科任务上结果欠佳。因此需要一种能更好地应对医学图像噪声和专门任务的模型架构。

Method: QuantumBoostNet采用混合经典-量子架构，包含一个经典骨干网络并配备两个头部：经典头与量子头，其中量子头由参数化的10量子比特量子电路实现。训练过程分两阶段进行，通过一个监控损失动态的混合参数实现两头部之间的自适应过渡。

Result: 在有限的量子比特可模拟条件下，QuantumBoostNet在心脏超声视图识别任务上持续优于最先进的经典模型及混合经典-量子模型，相较于最佳竞争对手实现了相对改进。该模型在已有图像分类基准上也展现出优越性能，并展现出对噪声的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持继续发展用于专科医学成像应用的混合经典-量子模型，QuantumBoostNet为这一方向提供了有效且鲁棒的架构选择。

Abstract: Accurate identification of the correct view or angle in cardiac ultrasound (echocardiogram) is a critical component of cardiologic imaging. This step is essential for precise anatomical interpretation, reliable measurement, and the reduction of clinical errors. Although computer vision has advanced significantly, most state-of-the-art models perform well on standard benchmarks but often yield suboptimal results in specialized medical imaging tasks due to the high level of noise present in the data. QuantumBoostNet, a hybrid classical-quantum architecture, is introduced to address these challenges. This model integrates a classical backbone with two heads: one classical and one quantum, with the quantum head implemented as a parametrized 10-qubit quantum circuit. Training occurs in two stages, with an adaptive transition between heads governed by a mixing parameter that monitors loss dynamics. Extensive experiments indicate that, despite the limited number of qubits that can be simulated, QuantumBoostNet consistently outperforms state-of-the-art classical and hybrid classical-quantum models in cardiac ultrasound view identification, achieving a relative improvement over the best competitor. QuantumBoostNet also demonstrates superior performance on established image classification benchmarks and exhibits robustness to noise. These findings support the continued development of hybrid classical-quantum models for specialized medical imaging applications.

</details>


### [288] [Understanding Evolution Strategies for LLM Reasoning: Broader Reasoning Coverage than GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27351)
*Yunpeng Ba,Zhi Zheng,Yue Xie,Jiaqing Li,Xialiang Tong,Tao Zhong,Mingxuan Yuan,Zhichao Lu,Xuyang Wu,Zhenkun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了进化策略（ES）作为大语言模型推理后训练范式的优化行为，发现ES在推理覆盖广度（Pass@K）上优于GRPO，同时揭示了ES的参数更新稀疏性和超参数设计规律，将ES定位为一种独特的推理后训练范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ES近期被提出作为LLM推理后训练的内存高效范式，但其优化行为尚未被深入研究，难以界定其相对于主流后训练范式（如GRPO）的优势范围。

Method: 通过系统研究ES的动态和机制：1）理论证明verifier投影的Jensen-Shannon多样性有助于更高Pass@K性能；2）提出顺序GRPO-ES训练策略结合两者优势；3）分析ES参数漂移中的功能稀疏性；4）研究超参数（种群大小）对ES效果的影响。

Result: 1）ES在Pass@K上优于GRPO，GRPO出现熵坍缩而ES同时提升Pass@1和更高的Pass@K；2）ES的任务性能增益仅来自稀疏子集的大幅更新，整体参数漂移不导致灾难性遗忘；3）更大的LLM需要更小的ES种群大小。

Conclusion: ES应被视为一种独特的推理后训练范式，而非GRPO的内存高效替代品，其价值体现在更广的推理覆盖度和功能稀疏性等独特属性上。

Abstract: Evolution Strategies (ES) have recently emerged as a memory-efficient post-training paradigm for LLM reasoning. However, the optimization behavior of ES remains understudied, making it hard to define its advantage scope compared to mainstream post-training paradigms (e.g., Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)). By systematically investigating ES dynamics and mechanisms, this paper first identifies a performance advantage of ES over GRPO, theoretically and empirically showing that ES can lead to broader reasoning coverage, thereby better exploiting the reasoning capabilities of pretrained LLMs. Theoretically, we show that verifier-projected Jensen-Shannon diversity across the ES population is helpful to higher Pass@K performances. Empirically, unlike GRPO, which exhibits entropy collapse, ES improves Pass@1 while attaining higher Pass@K than GRPO. We further develop a sequential GRPO-ES training strategy that combines GRPO's strength in Pass@1 with ES's gains in Pass@K. Second, we find that despite substantial whole-model parameter drift, the task-performance gains of ES are only contributed to a sparse subset of larger-magnitude updates. This functional sparsity suggests that large parameter movement need not imply widespread functional change, and held-out evaluations further show that it does not necessarily lead to catastrophic forgetting. Finally, we study how hyperparameter design affects the effectiveness of ES, demonstrating that ES requires a smaller population size in a larger LLM. These findings position ES as a distinct reasoning post-training paradigm rather than a less effective, memory-efficient alternative to GRPO.

</details>

### [289] [Comparative Evaluation of 3D Reconstruction Methods for Immersive Visualization of Laboratory Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27301)
*Brian De La Cruz,Aaron Y. Zhao,Maitrey Gramopadhye,Sawyer J. Lazar,Xianming Tan,Daniel Szafir,David S. Lawrence*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本研究比较了四种3D重建方法（摄影测量、NeRF、高斯泼溅和LiDAR）在生成教育用实验室物品全息模型方面的效果。研究生通过重复测量设计评估了形状、颜色、纹理和视觉缺陷等方面，发现NeRF方法在透明、反光或低纹理物体的高保真重建上表现最佳。研究还展示了面向AR/MR教育环境的沉浸式学习对象工作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索当前3D重建技术能否支持创建可用于教育目的的高保真实验室物品全息表示，为AR/MR教育环境中的沉浸式学习提供技术支持。

Method: 比较四种3D重建方法（摄影测量、NeRF、高斯泼溅、LiDAR），生成常见实验室物品的全息模型，由研究生采用重复测量设计对模型的形状、颜色、纹理和视觉缺陷进行评估。

Result: NeRF方法在所有物品上产生最一致的高保真表示，尤其在透明、反光或低纹理物品上优势明显；形状和颜色的重建成功率高于纹理，说明纹理仍具挑战性。

Conclusion: 研究不仅识别了各重建方法的优劣，还演示了创建沉浸式学习对象的实用工作流程，可支持实验前准备、空间推理和学生参与，为教育者和研究者在AR/MR教育环境开发中提供了设计相关的见解。

Abstract: In this study, we examined whether current 3D reconstruction methods can support the creation of realistic holographic representations of laboratory objects for educational use. In this regard, we compared four approaches: photogrammetry, a neural radiance field (NeRF)-based method, Gaussian splatting, and LiDAR. These methods were used to generate holographic models of common laboratory items and their fidelity was evaluated by graduate students. Participants assessed the models for shape, color, texture, and visual defects using a repeated-measures design. Across objects, the NeRF-based method produced the most consistently high-fidelity representations, particularly for transparent, reflective, or low-texture items that were difficult to capture with other approaches. Shape and color were generally reproduced more successfully than texture, suggesting that some visual properties remain more challenging to represent accurately in educational holograms. Beyond identifying the strengths and limitations of each reconstruction method, the study demonstrates a practical workflow for creating immersive learning objects that may support pre-laboratory preparation, spatial reasoning, and student engagement in AR/MR-based educational environments. These findings offer design-relevant insights for educators and researchers developing immersive digital learning experiences.

</details>

### [290] [Report of the 2026 Workshop on Next-Generation Ecosystems for Scientific Computing: Harnessing Community, Software, and AI for Cross-Disciplinary Team Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26519)
*Lois Curfman McInnes,Dorian Arnold,Prasanna Balaprakash,Mike Bernhardt,Franck Cappello,Beth Cerny,Deborah DiazGranados,Anshu Dubey,Nichole Etienne,Roscoe Giles,Diego Gomez-Zara,Denice Ward Hood,Mary Ann Leung,Vanessa Lopez-Marrero,Olivia B. Newton,Irene Qualters,Keita Teranishi,Stefan M. Wild,Gabrielle Allen,Richard Arthur,Alexandra Ballow,Tony Baylis,David E. Bernholdt,Daniel Bielich,Johanna Cohoon,Jeremy Crampton,Charles Ferenbaugh,Stephen M. Fiore,Thomas Herault,Tanzima Islam,Stephen Jacobsohn,Meifeng Lin,Charles Lively,Satoshi Matsuoka,Stasa Milojevic,Daniel Nichols,Chris Oehmen,Santiago Ospina Tabares,Michael E. Papka,Katherine Riley,Damian Rouson,Sudip K. Seal,Brittany Segundo,John Shalf,Andrew Siegel,Valerie Taylor,Jim Willenbring,Lou Woodley*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文是2026年下一代科学计算生态系统研讨会（三年系列的第二届）的综合报告，围绕AI赋能科学计算的社会技术协同设计，提炼出四大战略主题（软件生态系统、信任验证与可追溯性、人机协作与范式转变、劳动力/教学/治理），并将其转化为社区行动的八大优先事项，旨在构建可信、可持续、创新且韧性的科学计算生态系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学计算正经历由AI、异构计算、自动化和数据密集型研究推动的快速变革，不仅影响计算工具本身，还深刻改变了科研机构、劳动力模式与协作实践。现有生态系统设计缺乏对AI日益增长角色的系统性考虑，亟需通过社会技术协同设计来前瞻性地规划科学计算生态系统的未来。

Method: 基于2026年下一代科学计算生态系统研讨会（三年系列的第二届），通过多利益相关方研讨汇聚跨领域洞见，从社会技术协同设计视角识别四大相互关联的战略主题，并将各主题细化为对社区行动的八项优先事项，涵盖共享研究基础设施、信任与可追溯性、用户体验、人机配合、劳动力发展、跨部门协调、管理与可持续性以及科学价值评估。

Result: 提出八项社区行动优先事项：①共享研究基础设施；②信任与可追溯性；③用户体验；④人机配合；⑤劳动力发展；⑥跨部门协调；⑦管理与可持续性；⑧科学价值评估。这些优先事项覆盖了从技术基础设施到组织治理的全链条，为AI在科学工作中作用日益增强的情境下建设科学计算生态系统提供了行动方向框架。

Conclusion: 报告认为，通过落实这八项优先事项，可以推动建设值得信赖、可持续、创新且具备韧性的科学计算生态系统，以应对AI深度融入科学工作所带来的机遇与挑战，为未来三年系列的最终研讨会奠定基础。

Abstract: Scientific computing is undergoing rapid transformation as advances in artificial intelligence, heterogeneous computing, automation, and data-intensive research reshape not only computational tools but also the institutions, workforce models, and collaborative practices that support scientific discovery. This report synthesizes insights from the 2026 Workshop on Next-Generation Ecosystems for Scientific Computing, the second in a three-year series focused on strengthening scientific computing ecosystems through socio-technical co-design. Workshop discussions identified four interdependent strategic themes: software ecosystems for AI-enabled scientific discovery; trust, validation, and traceability; human-AI teaming and paradigm shifts; and workforce, pedagogy, and governance. The report translates these themes into eight priorities for community action spanning shared research infrastructure, trust and traceability, user experience, human-AI teaming, workforce development, cross-sector coordination, stewardship and sustainability, and evaluation of scientific value. Together, these priorities outline directions for building scientific computing ecosystems that remain trustworthy, sustainable, innovative, and resilient as AI assumes a growing role in scientific work.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

### [291] [Weight Distributions of Single Parity-Check Product Codes via Character Sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26457)
*Makson Miller Alves Ribeiro,Sara D. Cardell*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了二元单奇偶校验乘积码的结构与枚举性质，确定了SPC(m)⊗SPC(n)的广义Hamming重量谱，给出了SPC(n)⊗SPC(n)的最大码字重量和齐次重量枚举器的对称性条件，并通过MacWilliams恒等式的Walsh-Hadamard形式推导出重量枚举器的闭合表达式，利用Krawtchouk多项式给出了无需穷举的精确重量分布计算程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单奇偶校验乘积码是一类结构简单但具有重要理论意义的码类，其结构性质和重量分布对于纠错码理论及应用至关重要。此前对这类乘积码的广义Hamming重量谱、重量枚举器等问题研究尚不完善，本文旨在系统地解决这些问题。

Method: 采用组合与代数方法：首先确定乘积码的广义Hamming重量层级；对于方形乘积码，分析最大码字重量并考察齐次重量枚举器的对称性；通过对偶码的表征，利用MacWilliams恒等式的Walsh-Hadamard公式推导重量枚举器的闭合表达式；按辅助二元向量的Hamming重量分组，用二项式系数和交替卷积给出各项系数的显式公式；最后借助Krawtchouk多项式设计无需穷举所有码字的精确重量分布计算程序。

Result: 完整确定了SPC(m)⊗SPC(n)的广义Hamming重量层级；证明了SPC(n)⊗SPC(n)的齐次重量枚举器对称当且仅当n为偶数；推导出重量枚举器的精确闭合表达式，各系数以二项式系数和交替卷积显式表达；给出了基于Krawtchouk多项式的精确重量分布计算程序，并通过数值实例验证了公式的正确性。

Conclusion: 本文系统地解决了二元单奇偶校验乘积码的结构与枚举问题，包括广义Hamming重量谱、重量枚举器的闭合公式以及高效精确的重量分布计算算法，为这类码的理论研究和实际应用提供了完整的数学工具。

Abstract: We investigate structural and enumerative properties of binary single parity-check product codes. For each $n\geq 2$, $\operatorname{SPC}(n)$ denotes the binary single parity-check code of length $n$, consisting of all binary vectors of length $n$ having even Hamming weight. We determine the generalized Hamming weight hierarchy of the product code $\mathcal{C}_{m,n}=\operatorname{SPC}(m)\otimes\operatorname{SPC}(n)$, whose codewords can be represented as $m\times n$ binary matrices in which every row and every column has even Hamming weight. For the square product $\mathcal{C}_n =\operatorname{SPC}(n)\otimes\operatorname{SPC}(n)$, we also determine the maximum codeword weight and prove that its homogeneous weight enumerator is symmetric if and only if $n$ is even. After characterizing the dual code, we apply the MacWilliams identity in its Walsh--Hadamard formulation to derive an exact closed-form expression for the weight enumerator. By grouping the auxiliary binary vectors according to their Hamming weights, we obtain an explicit formula for each coefficient in terms of binomial coefficients and alternating convolutions. Finally, using Krawtchouk polynomials, we present an exact procedure for computing the full weight distribution without exhaustively enumerating all codewords. Numerical examples illustrate the formulas and verify the resulting computations.

</details>


### [292] [Decoding Algorithms for MDS Array Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26483)
*Sara D. Cardell,Gustavo Terra Bastos,Cintya Wink de Oliveira Benedito*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类由超正则矩阵与有限域上非奇异矩阵的Kronecker积构造的MDS阵列码的解码问题。利用其校验矩阵的特殊结构，针对不同信道模型开发了解码算法：擦除信道下可恢复任意n-k个符号擦除；q元对称信道下研究了单个和两个符号错误的定位与取值；并针对Vandermonde超正则矩阵的特殊情况利用其代数结构优化了解码过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MDS阵列码在分布式存储系统中具有重要应用，而由Kronecker积构造的这类阵列码虽然已有构造方法，但缺乏系统性的解码算法研究。本文旨在利用这类码校验矩阵的特殊结构，为不同信道模型设计高效和解码程序。

Method: 本文利用该类MDS阵列码校验矩阵由超正则矩阵与非奇异矩阵Kronecker积构成的结构特点：（1）对擦除信道，设计可恢复任意至多n-k个符号擦除的解码算法；（2）对q元对称信道，给出单个和两个符号错误的定位与取值判定程序；（3）针对超正则矩阵为Vandermonde矩阵的特例，进一步利用其代数结构简化解码过程。

Result: 给出了擦除信道下任意(n-k)个符号擦除的完整恢复算法；建立了q元对称信道下单符号和双符号错误的显式定位与纠错程序；并利用Vandermonde矩阵的结构给出了特殊情形下的高效解码方案，且在不同有限域上提供了实例验证。

Conclusion: 通过利用Kronecker积结构赋予校验矩阵的特殊性质，本文为该类MDS阵列码在擦除信道和q元对称信道下提供了系统化的解码方案，拓展了这类码字在实际存储与通信系统中的可用性。

Abstract: We study decoding procedures for a family of MDS array codes previously constructed from the Kronecker product of a superregular matrix and a non-singular matrix over a finite field. By exploiting the particular structure of their parity-check matrices, we develop decoding algorithms for different channel models. For the erasure channel, we provide an algorithm capable of recovering any pattern of up to $n-k$ symbol erasures. For the $q$-ary symmetric channel, we investigate the decoding of one and two symbol errors and give explicit procedures for determining their locations and values. We also consider the particular case in which the superregular matrix is a Vandermonde matrix, showing how its additional algebraic structure can be exploited in the decoding process. Explicit examples over different finite fields are provided to illustrate the proposed procedures.

</details>


### [293] [Sharp Minimax Regret for Infinite-Memory Logistic Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26515)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类具有无限输入记忆的外生驱动有限字母表在线预测问题。对滞后分解的频谱Γ_T(r)=Σlog(1+n_{T,j}r_j²)，证明了贝叶斯混合方法实现上界R_T(r)≤CΓ_T(r)，而在指数和多项式衰减包络条件下，通过Toeplitz设计证明了匹配的下界，从而确立了最小最大累积遗憾的精确标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解具有无限记忆的外生驱动源的在线预测复杂性，特别是不同滞后项对预测难度的影响如何累积，以及能否通过谱理论给出精确的最小最大遗憾率。

Method: 采用两套技术：(1) 上界：局部化贝叶斯混合方法，利用滞后解析的频谱Γ_T(r)实现遗憾上界；(2) 下界：Toeplitz设计构造对抗性/困难实例，在有限样本维数条件下证明遗憾下界与频谱匹配。此外还分析了截断策略的代价，并引入缩放在线牛顿预测器来达到频谱上界。

Result: 证明了Γ_T(r)是该源类在典型正则（指数与多项式包络）下的最小最大累积遗憾标度：指数包络r_j=Ae^{-αj}给出Θ(α⁻¹log²T)，多项式包络r_j=Aj^{-s}(s>1)给出Θ(T^{1/(2s)})。截断最近h个输入的代价为Σ_{j>h}n_{T,j}θ_j²，但同一最坏情况截断剖面可对应多项式级不同的遗憾。缩放在线牛顿预测器可达到频谱上界。

Conclusion: 滞后解析频谱Γ_T(r)精确刻画了此类外生滞后模型的最小最大遗憾，但该下界结论依赖于外生滞后结构，不能推广到任意的平稳无限记忆源（非纯剖面定理）。

Abstract: We study online prediction for a specific finite-alphabet, exogenously driven source with infinite input memory. Independent Rademacher inputs $(U_t)$ are observed sequentially, and the next binary mark has logit $\sum_{j=1}^{t}θ_jU_{t+1-j}$, where $\abs{θ_j}\leq r_j$ and $\sum_jr_j\leq B$. Regret is expected cumulative excess log loss. Lag $j$ can affect prediction by scale $r_j$ and enters only $n_{T,j}=T-j+1$ prediction rounds, leading to the lag-resolved spectrum $Γ_T(r)=\sum_{j=1}^{T}\log\!\left(1+n_{T,j}r_j^2\right)$. For every summable envelope, a localized Bayesian mixture proves $\cR_T(r)\leq CΓ_T(r)$. For exponential and polynomial envelopes, under the stated finite-sample dimension condition, a Toeplitz-design converse proves $\cR_T(r)\geq cΓ_T(r)$, with constants allowed to depend on the fixed decay parameters and the logit bound. Thus $Γ_T(r)$ is the minimax cumulative-regret scale for this source class in these canonical regimes, giving $Θ(α^{-1}\log^2T)$ for $r_j=Ae^{-αj}$ and $Θ(T^{1/(2s)})$ for $r_j=Aj^{-s}$, $s>1$. The converse is specific to the exogenous lagged model and is not a profile-only theorem for arbitrary stationary infinite-memory sources. Retaining only the most recent $h$ inputs costs order $\sum_{j>h}n_{T,j}θ_j^2$, yet the same worst-case truncation profile can correspond to polynomially different regret. A scaled online Newton predictor attains the spectrum upper bound.

</details>


### [294] [Spectral Approximation and Ergodic-Capacity Convergence of HMIMO Channels under Spatial-Wavenumber Domain Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26802)
*Hangsong Yan,Hong Yang,Shu Sun*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对连续全息大规模MIMO（HMIMO）信道建立了有限维谱逼近与遍历容量收敛的定量结果。在方形孔径和圆形波数支撑的约束下，空间-波数域失配导致不可分离的方形-圆盘集中问题，经典的可分离PSWF构造无法直接应用。作者将连续算子投影到一维PSWF的张量积子空间上，同时保持圆形波数支撑，得到一般非对角但高度稀疏的有限维矩阵。通过一维PSWF特征值尾部包络控制全谱逼近误差（包含保留特征值扰动和残差谱尾部），超过一维截断阈值后误差呈超指数衰减。进一步建立了连续信道与张量PSWF截断信道在各自发射协方差优化下的遍历容量间隙的非渐近上界，该边界继承了相同的超指数依赖。最后给出了显式径向和角度节点阈值的求积规则，数值结果表明基于空间自由度的常规截断可能遗漏性能相关模式，尤其在紧凑孔径下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续HMIMO信道的有限维近似是一个核心问题。经典的PSWF方法依赖可分离的正方形支撑，但物理上波数支撑是圆形的，导致空间-波数域失配，产生不可分离的方形-圆盘集中问题。已有方法因无法处理这种非可分离结构而不能直接应用，需要新的定量分析框架来刻画有限维截断的谱逼近误差和容量收敛性。

Method: 将连续积分算子投影到一维PSWF张量积子空间，同时保持圆形波数支撑约束，形成非对角但高度稀疏的有限维矩阵。利用一维PSWF特征值尾部包络来控制全谱逼近误差（包括保留特征值摄动和残余谱尾部）。基于谱分析结果导出遍历容量间隙的非渐近上界，并开发具有显式径向和角度节点阈值的求积规则用于矩阵求值。

Result: 全谱逼近误差由一维PSWF特征值尾部包络控制，超过一维截断阈值后误差呈超指数衰减。遍历容量间隙上界同样继承超指数依赖。在扁平化二维特征值排序下获得了渐进上包络。数值结果表明，基于空间自由度的常规截断会遗漏性能相关模式，尤其在紧凑孔径场景下。求积规则以显式径向和角度节点阈值高效实现。

Conclusion: 通过保持圆形波数支撑的张量PSWF投影，即使失去可分离性并引入非对角耦合，谱逼近和容量收敛仍可由一维PSWF尾部行为以超指数速度保证。这为连续HMIMO信道的有限维建模提供了严格的理论基础，并指出常规空间自由度截断的不足，特别是在紧凑孔径下需要更加精细的截断策略。

Abstract: We establish quantitative results on finite-dimensional spectral approximation and ergodic-capacity convergence for continuous Holographic Multiple-Input Multiple-Output (HMIMO) channels with square apertures and physically prescribed circular wavenumber support. The resulting spatial-wavenumber domain mismatch leads to a non-separable square-disk concentration problem for which the classical separable construction based on prolate spheroidal wave functions (PSWFs) cannot be directly applied. We project the continuous operator onto a tensor-product subspace of one-dimensional (1D) PSWFs while preserving the circular wavenumber support, yielding a generally non-diagonal but highly sparse finite-dimensional matrix. We show that the whole-spectrum approximation error, accounting for retained-eigenvalue perturbations and the residual spectral tail, remains controlled by a 1D PSWF eigenvalue-tail envelope despite the loss of separability and induced off-diagonal coupling. Beyond an explicit 1D truncation threshold, this error decays super-exponentially. This analysis further yields an asymptotic upper envelope for the eigenspectrum under the flattened two-dimensional eigenvalue ordering. We further establish a non-asymptotic upper bound on the gap between the actual ergodic capacities of the continuous and tensor-PSWF-truncated channels under their respective transmit-covariance optimizations. Combined with the spectral result, this capacity-gap bound inherits the same super-exponential dependence on the truncation order. Finally, quadrature rules with explicit radial and angular node thresholds are developed for evaluating the projected matrix. Numerical results show that conventional truncation based on spatial degrees of freedom can omit performance-relevant modes, particularly for compact apertures.

</details>


### [295] [Minimum Rate For Partially Observable Linear System with Side Information: LQG Plant and Gaussian-Markov Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26917)
*Sijie Li,Hyeji Kim*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了带有侧信息的部分可观测线性系统中，实现LQG控制所需的最小速率问题。证明了线性策略足以优化条件有向信息下界，并证明了标量情况下（时变和时不变系统）该优化问题是凸的，推广了以往仅考虑完全/部分观测或仅考虑完全观测加侧信息的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测的线性系统中，当存在侧信息（side information）时，如何确定通信或控制所需的最小速率是一个重要的理论问题。以往研究分别考虑了完全观测、部分观测以及完全观测加侧信息的情况，但尚未有工作统一处理部分可观测系统结合侧信息的场景，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文针对LQG被控对象和高斯-马尔可夫源，利用条件有向信息（conditional directed information）作为速率下界来刻画所需最小速率。作者证明了在部分可观测且带有侧信息的系统中，一类线性策略足以优化该条件有向信息下界；进一步引入该优化问题，并证明在标量情形下（无论时变还是时不变系统）该问题是凸优化问题，从而可高效求解。

Result: 证明了线性策略的最优性（即足以达到条件有向信息下界），并证明了标量系统的凸性，从而保证了优化问题的可解性。数值仿真展示了侧信息对部分可观测系统速率需求的影响，验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 本文的结果统一并推广了以往关于完全观测、部分观测以及带侧信息情景下的速率下界研究工作，表明从理论层面线性策略在这种广义场景下仍然是最优的，且标量情况下的优化问题具有良好结构（凸性），为实际系统的速率设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper studies the minimum rate required for a partially observable linear system with side information. The Linear Quadratic Gaussian(LQG) plant and the Gaussian-Markov source are considered. We show that a class of linear policies is sufficient for optimizing the conditional directed information lower bound. We also show that the resulting optimization problem is convex for the scalar case in both time-varying and time-invariant systems. Our results generalize the past works that consider the case with full or partial observation only, and the case with full observation and side information. Numerical simulations are presented to illustrate the effect of side information for partially observable systems.

</details>


### [296] [Real-Time Reconstruction of Markov Sources over MPR Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27116)
*Pansee S. Elessawy,Nikolaos Pappas*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究两个二进制马尔可夫源在一个具有多包接收（MPR）能力的共享无线信道上的实时重建与远程驱动问题。通过利用MPR而非丢弃同时传输，作者推导了重建误差（RTE）和驱动误差（CAE）的闭式表达式，并针对独立随机化策略提出了基于帕累托前沿的有限集搜索算法，同时以时间共享作为基准量化了独立随机化的性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于碰撞的信道模型会丢弃同时传输，未能利用MPR能力。本文旨在探索在共享无线信道上如何通过采样策略优化任务导向的通信目标（即重建误差和驱动误差），而非单纯的传输速率。

Method: 1）推导RTE和CAE关于有效更新概率的闭式表达式；2）利用可达更新率区域的几何结构，将独立随机化策略下的非凸优化问题转化为对帕累托高效策略的有限一维边界分支搜索；3）以时间共享（至多两个帕累托极端模式）作为基准，量化独立随机化的性能损失。

Result: 数值结果揭示：仅具备MPR能力并不足以改善任务导向目标；只有当两个源的同时接收均可靠时，同时解码才能提升性能；否则，避免同时传输的策略同样有效。

Conclusion: 独立随机化策略的最优解可通过有限搜索获得，其与时间共享基准的性能差距可被量化。MPR的优势取决于并发接收的可靠性，而非MPR本身的存在。

Abstract: This paper studies the real-time reconstruction and remote actuation of two binary Markov sources over a shared wireless channel with multi-packet reception (MPR). Unlike many existing collision-based formulations that discard simulta- neous transmissions, we exploit MPR and evaluate communica- tion through reconstruction and actuation errors rather than raw delivery rates. We consider two sensors observing the sources and aim to find sampling policies that minimize the weighted real- time reconstruction error (RTE), or equivalently the weighted cost of actuation error (CAE) for the considered binary sources, under per-sensor sampling constraints. We first obtain closed- form expressions for the RTE and CAE in terms of the effective update probabilities. When the sensors randomize independently, the MPR-induced update-rate map becomes bilinear, and the constrained optimization is nonconvex. We exploit the geometry of the achievable update-rate region to show that the search over Pareto-efficient independently randomized policies reduces to a finite set of one-dimensional boundary-branch searches with closed-form candidates. As a benchmark, we allow time sharing among joint sensor actions, and we prove that at most two Pareto- extreme modes are sufficient, and use this benchmark to quantify the loss caused by independent randomization. Numerical results reveal that MPR capability alone is not sufficient; simultaneous decoding improves the task-oriented objective only when con- current reception is reliable for both sources; otherwise, policies that avoid simultaneous transmissions can be equally effective.

</details>


### [297] [Knowledge Distillation Driven Semantic NOMA with GAN Refinement for 6G Robotic Vehicle Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27198)
*Qifei Wang,Zhen Gao,Li Qiao,Ziwei Wan,De Mi,Dapeng Li,Ying Sun*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出KDG-SemNOMA框架，结合知识蒸馏与生成模型，解决6G赋能的NOMA机器人车辆网络中上行语义通信的干扰与能耗问题，在FFHQ-256数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G赋能的机器人车辆（RVs）需要高保真视觉感知，但面临严格的带宽和能量约束。语义通信虽提供频谱高效的解决方案，但在上行NOMA RV网络中遭受严重干扰，亟需一种鲁棒且绿色的通信框架。

Method: 1) 开发基于ConvNeXt的深度联合信源信道编码（DeepJSCC）架构，并集成增强注意力特征（AF）模块以支持动态信道自适应；2) 采用两阶段知识蒸馏策略，利用正交传输教师模型指导NOMA学生模型，在不增加推理开销的情况下缓解干扰；3) 引入通道条件GAN（cGAN），将Stage-I初始重建和信道状态作为条件输入，细化粗糙输出为具有真实纹理的高保真图像。

Result: 在FFHQ-256数据集上的实验表明，KDG-SemNOMA在像素级准确性和感知保真度方面均显著优于最先进的方法。

Conclusion: KDG-SemNOMA通过知识蒸馏与生成模型增强相结合，有效解决了NOMA RV网络中的干扰问题与像素级优化的过平滑伪影，实现了鲁棒且绿色的机器人车辆语义通信。

Abstract: To achieve sustainable intelligent mobility, 6G-empowered robotic vehicles (RVs) require high-fidelity visual perception under stringent bandwidth and energy constraints. Semantic communication offers a spectral-efficient solution but suffers from severe interference in uplink non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA) RV networks. To address this, we propose a knowledge distillation-driven and generative models-enhanced NOMA framework for robust and green RV communications, named KDG-SemNOMA. First, we develop a ConvNeXt-based deep joint source-channel coding (DeepJSCC) architecture with an enhanced attention feature (AF) module for dynamic channel adaptation. Second, to mitigate interference without inference overhead, an orthogonal transmission teacher model guides the NOMA student model via a two-stage knowledge distillation strategy. Finally, to address the over-smoothing artifacts of pixel-wise optimization, we introduce a channel-conditional GAN (cGAN). By explicitly taking the Stage-I initial reconstruction and channel states as conditional inputs, this module refines coarse outputs into high-fidelity images with realistic textures. Experiments on FFHQ-256 demonstrate that KDG-SemNOMA significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both pixel-level accuracy and perceptual fidelity.

</details>


### [298] [Deterministic Identification over Additive Gaussian Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27243)
*Jonathan E. W. Huffmann,Holger Boche*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对加性高斯信道（包括加性白高斯信道）的确定性识别容量问题，通过引入格子理论（lattice theory）的新视角，首次建立了该容量的紧致界，填补了确定性识别在加性高斯信道下容量未知的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后Shannon信息理论中，确定性识别作为一种新兴通信范式，在实现复杂度和识别速率增益之间取得了平衡，对未来通信系统（包括分子通信）具有重要应用前景。然而，即使是最简单的加性白高斯信道，其确定性识别容量至今仍属未知，这限制了该理论的实际部署与性能评估。

Method: 本文引入了一种关于确定性识别的新视角，将问题与格子理论建立联系，利用格子理论中的数学工具来推导确定性识别容量的上界和下界，从而获得紧致界。

Result: 建立了加性高斯信道上确定性识别容量的紧致界（tight bounds），首次确定了该容量的上界与下界之间的紧致关系。

Conclusion: 通过格子理论的引入，解决了加性高斯信道确定性识别容量长期未知的问题，为确定性识别在加性高斯信道下的理论完备性和实际应用提供了坚实基础，进一步巩固了其作为未来通信系统（包括分子通信）可用方案的可行性。

Abstract: Modern communication systems impose strict demands on data rate,
  reliability, and power efficiency. In this context, emerging communication
  paradigms such as identification via channels have become an important topic
  in post-Shannon information theory, offering the potential for substantially
  higher identification rates than in conventional channel coding.Deterministic
  identification is particularly interesting for specialized communication
  scenarios because it provides a balance between implementation complexity and
  the communication gains due to higher identification rates. It is therefore a
  promising communication scheme for future communication systems, including
  molecular communication systems.
  Additive Gaussian channels, particularly the additive white Gaussian channel,
  are among the most important channel models for analyzing the performance of
  communication systems in information and communication theory. This importance
  stems from both their mathematical tractability and their ubiquitous
  appearance in practical applications.
  To date the deterministic identification capacity for additive Gaussian
  channels remains unknown even for the simplest case of the additive white
  Gaussian channel. In this paper, we establish tight bounds on the
  deterministic identification capacity of additive Gaussian channels by
  introducing a new perspective on deterministic identification. To this end, we
  apply results from lattice theory to obtain new capacity results.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [299] [Simultaneous Envy and Equitability Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26410)
*Hadi Hosseini,Shraddha Pathak,Lirong Xia,Chengkai Zhang*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究公平分配中两个根本不同公平概念（无嫉妒EF和公平性EQ）的兼容性，考察在不可分物品和家务两种场景下同时满足其松弛版本的可行性与复杂度，并首次引入跨概念的ex-ante-ex-post保证研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平分配研究要么关注同时满足密切相关的公平概念，要么在ex-ante和ex-post世界中实现单一概念，但缺乏对本质不同公平概念（如无嫉妒与公平性）兼容性的研究。

Method: 分别研究goods-only和chores-only两类设置中EF1+EQ1及更强松弛的存在性和算法复杂度；构造归一化可加性估值的反例证明EF1+EQ1可能不存在；设计针对最多7个agent的归一化二元goods的EF1+EQ1分配算法；并推广到跨概念（一个概念在ex-ante、另一个在ex-post）的随机化分配保证研究。

Result: 证明了EF1+EQ1在归一化可加估值下可能不存在；对归一化二元goods，设计出最多支持7个agent的EF1+EQ1分配算法；对二元chores，任何agent数量下（无需归一化）都能保证更强的EFX+EQX；两类设置之间存在显著对比。

Conclusion: 不同公平概念在goods和chores设置中的兼容性存在鲜明对比：chores比goods更容易同时满足更强的公平松弛；同时开辟了跨概念ex-ante-ex-post保证这一新研究方向。

Abstract: Recent work in fair division has focused on either simultaneously satisfying closely related fairness notions or achieving a single notion across the ex-ante and ex-post worlds. We study the compatibility of two fundamentally different fairness notions: envy-freeness and equitability. For indivisible goods-only and chores-only settings, we study the existence and complexity of simultaneously satisfying their relaxations, revealing sharp contrasts between the two settings. We show that EF1+EQ1 may fail to exist even for normalized, additive valuations. Our main algorithmic result computes an EF1+EQ1 allocation for normalized binary goods with at most seven agents. In sharp contrast, binary chores admit the stronger EFX+EQX guarantee for any number of agents, even without normalization. We further initiate the study of cross-notion ex-ante--ex-post guarantees, asking whether randomized allocations can provide ex-ante guarantees for one notion while preserving ex-post guarantees for another.

</details>


### [300] [Trusting AI in Competitive Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26539)
*Jussi Keppo,Yuze Li,Gerry Tsoukalas,Nuo Yuan*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 通过实验室实验（273名卖家、91个三方市场、30轮）研究寡头定价中人们对AI建议信任的动态分歧，发现市场性别构成显著影响建议的采纳与效果：全女性市场中AI建议使价格提升29%、利润提升39%，而全男性和混合性别市场无显著效果；非齐性隐马尔可夫模型揭示性别构成特定的动态信任学习机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们在使用AI建议时信任会分化——有人加深信任、有人逐渐失去信任。现有研究缺乏对这一分歧在"建议无法自证"的寡头定价情境下的理解，且忽视了市场性别构成这一社会因素如何塑造人机信任的动态演变。

Method: 实验室实验方法：273名卖家在91个三人市场中竞争30轮，实验操纵AI定价建议的存在与否以及市场性别构成（全女性、全男性、混合）。使用非齐性隐马尔可夫模型（Non-Homogeneous Hidden Markov Model）分析建议采纳的动态模式。

Result: 全女性市场中AI建议使价格提高29%、利润提高39%；全男性和混合性别市场无显著影响。动态分析发现盈利轮次在全女性市场中预测建议采纳上升，在其他市场中预测采纳下降，呈现"习得信任与自利归因"的差异模式。该结果与近期关于性别与AI的现有证据相反。

Conclusion: 平台治理与监管不应只关注算法本身，还应关注塑造算法效果的人类因素（如性别构成）；AI建议的效果取决于社会情境而非算法自身。

Abstract: Problem definition: People's trust in AI advice diverges as they use it, deepening for some and eroding for others. We study this divergence in oligopoly pricing, where advice cannot prove itself: rivals' responses decide whether it pays off. Methodology/results: In a laboratory experiment, 273 sellers compete across 91 three-seller markets over 30 rounds; we vary the presence of AI pricing recommendations and the gender composition of the market (female-only, male-only, or mixed). We find that the gender composition of the market shapes how sellers learn from the advice, and where prices settle as a result. In female-only markets, recommendations raise prices by 29% and profits by 39%; in male-only and mixed-gender markets, they have no significant effect. A Non-Homogeneous Hidden Markov Model reveals a composition-specific dynamic association: profitable rounds predict rising adherence to the AI in female-only markets and declining adherence otherwise, a pattern consistent with learned trust and self-serving attribution. The pattern reverses what recent evidence on gender and AI would predict. Managerial implications: We discuss implications for platform governance and regulatory oversight, which should focus not only on the algorithm but on the human side that shapes its effects.

</details>


### [301] [A lone divider allocation algorithm with subjective divisibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26801)
*Uriel Feige*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究主观可分性分配模型中的最大最小份额（MMS）分配问题，将此前已知的5/9-MMS分配的存在性改进为3/5-MMS分配的存在性，证明基于对lone divider框架的适应性修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在主观可分性分配模型中，商品被分为两类：一类可按任意比例分配（可分数量的商品），另一类只有整个商品（完整份额）才有价值。此前已有工作证明该模型中5/9-MMS分配的存在性，本文旨在改进这一近似比，进一步逼近最优MMS分配。

Method: 本文通过对经典的lone divider框架（孤立分割者框架）进行适应性调整和扩展，构造了一种新的证明方法，以证明在主观可分性分配模型中3/5-MMS分配的存在性。核心思想是利用lone divider协议的结构优势来处理混合了可分商品和不可分商品的复杂分配场景。

Result: 成功证明了在主观可分性分配模型中，3/5-MMS分配总是存在，将此前已知的5/9（约0.556）近似比提升至3/5（即0.6），取得了显著的改进。

Conclusion: 本文通过改编lone divider框架，证明了主观可分性分配模型中的3/5-MMS分配存在性，改进了已有结果，为该模型下MMS分配的研究提供了更强的理论保证。

Abstract: In the subjective divisibility allocation model, all goods are divisible, every agent $i$ has a non-negative additive valuation function $v_i$, where for some goods $v_i$ is additive over fractions of the good, and for some goods, any fraction smaller than~1 has value~0. Previous work established the existence of $\frac{5}{9}$-MMS allocations in this setting. We show that $\frac{3}{5}$-MMS allocations exist. Our proof is based on an adaptation of the lone divider framework.

</details>


### [302] [Robust Lottery Compression for Metric Voting: A Transfer Principle for Bounded Randomness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26854)
*Jianhao Jia,Bo Peng*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究有界随机性下的度量失真问题：投票规则必须确定性地输出一个包含K个候选人的多重集，再均匀随机选择。作者证明了对于任意K≥802，存在一个有界随机性规则，其失真不超过5/2 + O(K^{-1/3})，逼近当前无限制随机化的最优上界5/2。此外，164个条目即可打破确定性3的障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机化社会选择中，度量失真是一个核心衡量标准。此前研究表明，在"有界随机性"（先确定性选出K个候选人的多重集再均匀随机选一个）的限制下，可以击败最优确定性失真3。本文试图回答：这种受限模型究竟能逼近无限制随机化（当前最优上界5/2）到何种程度，以及打破确定性障碍所需的最小列表规模。

Method: 核心技术创新是一个"无维度压缩定理"：若一个彩票（lottery）失真不超过ρ，且其支撑中每个候选人的确定性失真不超过H，则该彩票可用一个均匀K条目近似，其失真不超过ρ + (H+1)√(π/(8K))。对于Mixed Integrated Veto规则，由于不满足支撑条件，作者先移除早期被淘汰的结果，以O(τ²)的失真代价换取支撑中每个候选人的确定性失真O(1/τ)上界，再与压缩定理结合，平衡修复代价与压缩损失得到O(K^{-1/3})的收敛率。

Result: 1) 对于任意整数K≥802，存在有界随机性规则，失真至多为 5/2 + 3(π/(8K))^{1/3} + 2√(π/(8K))，即O(ε^{-3})个条目即可达到5/2+ε的失真。2) 164个条目即可实现严格小于3的失真，给出最小列表规模2 ≤ N* ≤ 164以打破确定性障碍。3) 该结果独立于选民和候选人的数量。

Conclusion: 有界随机性模型几乎可以达到无限制随机化的最优失真上界5/2，且所需的条目数与选民和候选人数无关。压缩定理为理解彩票式投票规则的压缩损失提供了新的理论工具，但N*的下界（的最小值）仍有待进一步收窄。

Abstract: We study metric distortion in randomized social choice under bounded randomness: on every preference profile, the voting rule must deterministically identify a multiset of $K$ candidates and then select a uniformly random entry. Previous work showed that this restricted model can beat the optimal deterministic distortion of $3$. We show that it can in fact approach the current best unrestricted upper benchmark of $5/2$. For every integer $K\ge 802$, there exists a bounded-randomness rule with distortion at most $\frac{5}{2} +3\left(\fracπ{8K}\right)^{1/3} +2\sqrt{\fracπ{8K}}$.
  Consequently, $O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ entries suffice for distortion $5/2+\varepsilon$, independently of the numbers of voters and candidates. We also show that $164$ entries already achieve distortion strictly below $3$, giving $2\le N^\star\le 164$ for the minimum list size needed to break the deterministic barrier. Our main technical contribution is a dimension-free compression theorem: if a lottery has distortion at most $ρ$ and every candidate in its support has deterministic distortion at most $H$, then it admits a uniform $K$-entry approximation with distortion at most $ρ+(H+1)\sqrt{π/(8K)}$. Thus, lotteries whose possible outcomes are already well behaved incur only $O(K^{-1/2})$ compression loss. Mixed Integrated Veto does not satisfy this support condition, so we first remove early-eliminated outcomes, trading $O(τ^2)$ distortion loss for an $O(1/τ)$ bound on the deterministic distortion of every supported candidate. Balancing this repair cost against compression yields the $O(K^{-1/3})$ convergence rate.

</details>


### [303] [Blindfolded pursuit with delays of your choice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27347)
*Torben Schürenberg,Maximilian J. Stahlberg*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了单个追踪者与不可见逃跑者在图上的追逃博弈。追踪者可为图的边分配整数旅行时间，并指定有限顶点查询序列；逃跑者选择一条可行走路径以躲避所有查询。研究表明，赋予追踪者选择旅行时间的能力后，单个追踪者即可在任意图上以多项式时间获胜，这与无权图中需要线性数量猎人/消防员的情形形成鲜明对比。若允许逃跑者等待且可从未知较早时间出发，追踪者仍可在指数时间内取胜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的猎人与兔子博弈及消防博弈中，无权图设置下需要多个猎人或消防员才能保证成功捕获/扑灭，其数量可随顶点数线性增长。作者希望探究赋予单个追踪者调整边权（旅行时间）的能力是否能弥补人数上的不足，即用"时间操控"替代"数量优势"，从而在任意图上实现单智能体高效求解。

Method: 作者将两种博弈统一在带权边旅行时间框架下处理：(1) 猎人-兔子博弈（逃跑者每步必须移动）：通过分配整数边权并设计查询序列，使单个追踪者可在多项式时间内保证获胜，算法基于对图结构的加工与时间控制；(2) 消防博弈（逃跑者可等待）：将问题转化为燃烧图熄灭过程，同样借助可控旅行时间实现多项式时间单消防员成功扑灭；(3) 对逃跑者可等待且起始时刻未知的更强博弈模型，证明追踪者可在指数时间内获胜。

Result: 核心结果为：在有权图设置下，单个追踪者通过选择边旅行时间即可在任意图上以多项式时间获胜（两个基本博弈变体）；在逃跑者起始时间未知的更一般设置下，单追踪者仍可保证在指数时间内获胜。这与无权图中所需猎人数/消防员数随顶点数线性增长的下界形成明显反差，凸显了边权调控对追逃博弈搜索复杂度的决定性影响。

Conclusion: 本文确立了一个关键洞察：追踪者选择旅行时间的能力本质上是将"空间并行性"转化为"时间灵活性"，从而以单个智能体替代多个智能体完成搜索/灭火任务。未来工作可关注：时间复杂度的最优下界、部分可观测情形下的近似策略、以及该框架在安全监控、网络入侵检测等实际场景中的应用推广。

Abstract: We study pursuit-evasion games on graphs with a single pursuer and an invisible evader. The pursuer may assign integer travel times to the edges of the graph and specify a finite sequence of vertices to query, one per time step. The evader then chooses a walk over the same time horizon, aiming to elude all queries. With unit travel times, the setting in which the evader must move at every time step is known as the hunter and rabbit game, while the variant in which the evader can wait at a vertex can be phrased as a firefighting game: the vertices of a burning graph must be extinguished, and any vertex left burning reignites its neighbors. For both settings, we show that the power to choose travel times allows a single pursuer to succeed in polynomial time on any graph. This contrasts with unweighted graphs, where the number of hunters or firefighters needed can grow linearly in the number of vertices. If the evader, in addition to waiting, may start at an earlier time unknown to the pursuer, we show that the pursuer still wins on any graph given exponential time.

</details>


### [304] [Token-Level Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27382)
*Hanbing Liu,Bowei Zhang,Changyuan Yu,Yinyu Ye,Qi Qi*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 提出LAMA（Latent Advertiser Mixture Auction），一种面向生成式AI的原生广告拍卖机制，将广告主影响力直接嵌入token级生成过程，实现马尔可夫激励兼容、个体理性及近似最优的KL正则化社会福利，并通过学习式实现与真实商业搜索实验验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI正在改变人们获取信息的方式，对传统围绕预定义广告位构建的广告机制构成挑战。现有广告范式基于固定广告槽位，无法适应token级、动态生成的广告展示方式，因此需要一种能够直接嵌入生成过程的"生成原生"广告机制。

Method: LAMA将广告主广告主报告局部延续价值（local continuation values），诱导产生广告主特定的下一token策略；平台通过潜在混合（latent mixture）解码并更新分配后验。机制满足Markov DSIC（激励兼容）和IR（个体理性），并实现近似最优的KL正则化社会福利。此外，开发了基于学习的实现方案，从学习的局部优势和根值在线重构所需的广告主报告。

Result: 在真实商业搜索查询分区的概念验证实验中，LAMA在维持面向用户响应质量的同时，提升了平台社会福利和收入，初步证明了生成原生广告机制的可行性。

Conclusion: LAMA为生成原生广告提供了理论完备且实践可行的token级拍卖机制，在激励兼容性、社会福利与商业收益之间取得良好平衡，是生成式AI时代广告机制设计的重要探索方向。

Abstract: Generative AI is transforming how people access information, challenging traditional advertising mechanisms built around predefined slots. Towards generation-native advertising, we propose the Latent Advertiser Mixture Auction (LAMA), a token-level advertising mechanism that embeds advertiser influence directly into the generation process. Advertisers report local continuation values that induce advertiser-specific next-token policies, from which the platform decodes through a latent mixture while updating an allocation posterior. We show that LAMA satisfies Markov DSIC and IR, and achieves near-optimal KL-regularized welfare. We further develop a learning-based implementation that reconstructs the required reports online from learned local advantages and root values. Proof-of-concept experiments on real-world commercial-search query splits show that LAMA improves platform welfare and revenue while maintaining user-facing response quality, providing initial evidence for the feasibility of generation-native advertising.

</details>


### [305] [Algorithms for Robbins' Problem using Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27419)
*Léonard Brice,F. Thomas Bruss,Anirban Majumdar,Jean-François Raskin*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究Robbins问题（秘书选择问题的完全信息变体），将其建模为无限马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），并提出若干有限状态抽象来近似该问题的值。作者为所有5≤n≤100的候选人数量提供了比以往更好的近似值，此前仅对n≤4有精确值，且数值近似仅适用于个位数级别的n。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Robbins问题长期以来缺乏有效的近似方法，精确值仅对n≤4已知，数值近似也只覆盖了很小的n值。由于该问题需要完整记忆过往候选人的值才能实现最优期望秩最小化，使得分析极具挑战性，因此需要新的建模与求解方法。

Method: 作者首先将Robbins问题实例建模为无限马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），随后提出多种有限状态抽象来近似这些MDP的值。通过识别简单但足够有效的记忆结构，在n固定时获得接近最优的选择策略。

Result: 对于所有5≤n≤100的候选人数量，作者给出了比此前已知结果更好的近似值，填补了中等规模n值下Robbins问题缺乏良好近似的空白。

Conclusion: 尽管完整记忆对于最优期望秩最小化是必要的，但作者证明了简单的记忆结构足以获得接近最优的选择策略，并通过有限状态MDP抽象有效扩展了Robbins问题可解的规模范围。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider Robbins' problem, which is a full information variant of the well-known secretary selection problem.
